<file path="src/db/deployment.py">
"""Reproducible schema and real-data migration. Application code is never imported."""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import math
import os
import re
import sys
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
CATALOG = json.loads((ROOT/'src/db/catalog.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))

REGULATION_TABLES = ['hr_regulation_pilot.institution', 'hr_regulation_pilot.regulation', 'hr_regulation_pilot.regulation_version', 'hr_regulation_pilot.regulation_file', 'hr_regulation_pilot.regulation_node', 'hr_regulation_pilot.regulation_scope', 'hr_regulation_pilot.regulation_reference', 'hr_regulation_pilot.rag_chunk', 'hr_regulation_pilot.rag_chunk_source']
EVALUATION_TABLES = ['hr_regulation_pilot.evaluation_result_schema', 'hr_regulation_pilot.evaluation_item_definition', 'hr_regulation_pilot.evaluation_schema_source']
PACKAGE = ROOT / 'data' / 'regulations' / '한국수자원공사_규정_통합전처리_V01'

# API fields absent in a response must be representable as unknown, not fabricated.
INGESTION_NULLABLE = {'public.courses': ('cost', 'is_matchup', 'difficulty'),
                      'public.certifications': ('provider',)}
for _table, _columns in INGESTION_NULLABLE.items():
    for _column in CATALOG[_table]['columns']:
        if _column['column'] in _columns:
            _column['not_null'] = False
            _column['default'] = None



# Canonical source content travels with the five master tables on export/bootstrap.
CONTENT_COLUMNS = {}
for _name in ('courses','books','certifications','jobs','majors'):
    _extra = {'source_url':'text', 'content_status':'text', 'source_verified_at':'timestamp with time zone'}
    if _name != 'courses':
        _extra['description'] = 'text'
    CONTENT_COLUMNS['public.'+_name] = _extra
    for _field, _type in _extra.items():
        if not any(c['column']==_field for c in CATALOG['public.'+_name]['columns']):
            CATALOG['public.'+_name]['columns'].append({'column':_field,'type':_type,
                'default':None,'identity':'','generated':'','not_null':False})


def apply_ingestion_schema(conn):
    """Idempotent non-destructive extension for current and newly bootstrapped DBs."""
    for table, columns in CONTENT_COLUMNS.items():
        for column, kind in columns.items():
            conn.execute(f'ALTER TABLE {table} ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS {column} {kind}')
    for table, columns in INGESTION_NULLABLE.items():
        for column in columns:
            conn.execute(f'ALTER TABLE {table} ALTER COLUMN {column} DROP NOT NULL, ALTER COLUMN {column} DROP DEFAULT')


MIGRATIONS = ROOT/'src/db/migrations'
PILOT = ROOT/'data/regulations/인재개발규정_시범_V01'
REFERENCE_NAMES = {'skill_tags', 'courses', 'books', 'certifications', 'jobs', 'majors',
                   'course_skill_map', 'book_skill_map', 'cert_skill_map', 'job_skill_map', 'major_skill_map'}
TRANSFER_TABLES = {t for t in CATALOG if t.startswith('hr_regulation_pilot.') or t in {'public.'+n for n in REFERENCE_NAMES}}


def ident(value):
    if not re.fullmatch(r'[a-z_][a-z0-9_]*', value):
        raise ValueError('Unsupported SQL identifier')
    return '"'+value+'"'


def table_sql(table):
    if table not in CATALOG:
        raise ValueError(f'Unknown table: {table}')
    return '.'.join(ident(part) for part in table.split('.'))


def digest(path):
    return hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()


def inside(directory, relative):
    root = directory.resolve()
    result = (root/relative).resolve()
    if not result.is_relative_to(root) or result == root:
        raise ValueError('Path escapes the selected data directory')
    return result


def table_order(tables):
    pending, ordered = set(tables), []
    while pending:
        ready = sorted(t for t in pending if not any(
            fk['target'] in pending and fk['target'] != t for fk in CATALOG[t]['fks']))
        if not ready:
            raise ValueError('Cross-table foreign-key cycle in transfer set')
        ordered.extend(ready)
        pending.difference_update(ready)
    return ordered


def row_order(table, rows):
    spec = CATALOG[table]
    keys = spec['pk']
    by_key = {tuple(r[k] for k in keys): r for r in rows}
    if len(by_key) != len(rows):
        raise ValueError(f'Duplicate primary keys in {table}')
    self_fks = [f for f in spec['fks'] if f['target'] == table]
    if not self_fks:
        return rows
    dependencies = {key: set() for key in by_key}
    for fk in self_fks:
        target_columns = fk['target_columns']
        target_index = {tuple(row[c] for c in target_columns): key for key, row in by_key.items()}
        for key, row in by_key.items():
            parent = tuple(row[c] for c in fk['columns'])
            if any(v is None for v in parent):
                continue
            parent_key = target_index.get(parent)
            if parent_key is not None and parent_key != key:
                dependencies[key].add(parent_key)
    pending, ordered = dict(by_key), []
    while pending:
        ready = [k for k in pending if not (dependencies[k] & pending.keys())]
        if not ready:
            raise ValueError(f'Self-reference cycle in {table}')
        for key in ready:
            ordered.append(pending.pop(key))
    return ordered

def validate_rows(table, rows):
    spec = CATALOG[table]
    expected = {c['column'] for c in spec['columns']}
    for row in rows:
        for field in CONTENT_COLUMNS.get(table, {}):
            row.setdefault(field, None)  # pre-extension transfer bundles
        if set(row) != expected:
            raise ValueError(f'Column set mismatch in {table}')
        for col in spec['columns']:
            value = row[col['column']]
            if col.get('not_null') and value is None:
                raise ValueError(f"NULL in {table}.{col['column']}")
            if col['type'].startswith('vector(') and value is not None:
                vec = json.loads(value) if isinstance(value, str) else value
                dim = int(re.search(r'\d+', col['type'])[0])
                if not isinstance(vec, list) or len(vec) != dim or not all(isinstance(v, (int, float)) and math.isfinite(v) for v in vec):
                    raise ValueError(f"Invalid vector in {table}.{col['column']}")
    return row_order(table, rows)


def connect(env_name):
    try:
        import psycopg
        from psycopg.rows import dict_row
        from dotenv import load_dotenv
    except ImportError as exc:
        raise RuntimeError('Install requirements-db.txt before connecting.') from exc
    load_dotenv(ROOT/'.env', override=False)
    url = os.getenv(env_name)
    if not url:
        available = [name for name in ('SUPABASE_DB_URL','SOURCE_DB_URL','TARGET_DB_URL') if os.getenv(name)]
        hint = (' Existing DB: omit --db-env to use SUPABASE_DB_URL. A new destination requires its own URL; '
                'do not alias TARGET_DB_URL to the source.') if env_name == 'TARGET_DB_URL' else ''
        raise ValueError(f'Missing environment variable: {env_name}. Configured names: {available}.{hint}')
    try:
        return psycopg.connect(url, connect_timeout=10, sslmode=os.getenv('DB_SSLMODE','require'),
                               application_name='growpath_db_deployment', row_factory=dict_row)
    except psycopg.OperationalError:
        raise RuntimeError(f'DB connection failed. Check {env_name}, host access and SSL settings.') from None



def normalize_type(value):
    value = value.replace('extensions.', '').replace('public.', '').lower()
    value = value.replace('character varying', 'varchar')
    value = value.replace('integer', 'int')
    return value


def check_schema(conn, allow_missing=False):
    errors = []
    for table, spec in CATALOG.items():
        schema, name = table.split('.')
        cols = conn.execute('''SELECT a.attname AS name, format_type(a.atttypid,a.atttypmod) AS type
            FROM pg_attribute a JOIN pg_class c ON c.oid=a.attrelid
            JOIN pg_namespace n ON n.oid=c.relnamespace
            WHERE n.nspname=%s AND c.relname=%s AND a.attnum>0 AND NOT a.attisdropped''', (schema,name)).fetchall()
        found = {c['name']: c['type'] for c in cols}
        for col in spec['columns']:
            field = col['column']
            if field not in found:
                if not allow_missing:
                    errors.append(f'{table}.{field}: missing')
            elif normalize_type(found[field]) != normalize_type(col['type']):
                errors.append(f"{table}.{field}: {found[field]} != {col['type']}")
        if not found or allow_missing:
            continue
        key_rows = conn.execute('''SELECT con.contype, rn.nspname AS target_schema, rc.relname AS target_table,
            ARRAY(SELECT a.attname FROM unnest(con.conkey) WITH ORDINALITY AS k(num, ord)
                  JOIN pg_attribute a ON a.attrelid=con.conrelid AND a.attnum=k.num ORDER BY k.ord) AS columns,
            ARRAY(SELECT a.attname FROM unnest(con.confkey) WITH ORDINALITY AS k(num, ord)
                  JOIN pg_attribute a ON a.attrelid=con.confrelid AND a.attnum=k.num ORDER BY k.ord) AS target_columns
            FROM pg_constraint con JOIN pg_class c ON c.oid=con.conrelid
            JOIN pg_namespace n ON n.oid=c.relnamespace
            LEFT JOIN pg_class rc ON rc.oid=con.confrelid LEFT JOIN pg_namespace rn ON rn.oid=rc.relnamespace
            WHERE n.nspname=%s AND c.relname=%s AND con.contype IN ('p','f')''',(schema,name)).fetchall()
        if not any(k['contype']=='p' and k['columns']==spec['pk'] for k in key_rows):
            errors.append(f'{table}: primary key mismatch')
        for fk in spec['fks']:
            if not any(k['contype']=='f' and k['columns']==fk['columns'] and
                       f"{k['target_schema']}.{k['target_table']}"==fk['target'] and k['target_columns']==fk['target_columns'] for k in key_rows):
                errors.append(f"{table}: missing foreign key {fk['columns']}")
    if errors:
        raise ValueError('Schema mismatch; no automatic destructive conversion:\n'+'\n'.join(errors))


def migrate(conn):
    conn.execute('SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(763250130)')
    check_schema(conn, allow_missing=True)
    conn.execute('CREATE SCHEMA IF NOT EXISTS growpath_meta')
    conn.execute('''CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS growpath_meta.schema_migrations (
        version text PRIMARY KEY, sha256 text NOT NULL, applied_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now())''')
    applied = {r['version']: r['sha256'] for r in conn.execute('SELECT version,sha256 FROM growpath_meta.schema_migrations').fetchall()}
    done = []
    for path in sorted(MIGRATIONS.glob('*.sql')):
        version, checksum = path.name, digest(path)
        if version in applied:
            if applied[version] != checksum:
                raise ValueError(f'Applied migration changed: {version}')
            continue
        conn.execute(path.read_text(encoding='utf-8'), prepare=False)
        conn.execute('INSERT INTO growpath_meta.schema_migrations(version,sha256) VALUES (%s,%s)', (version,checksum))
        done.append(version)
    apply_ingestion_schema(conn)
    check_schema(conn)
    # Ask Supabase's PostgREST to refresh after commit. Ordinary PostgreSQL accepts NOTIFY too.
    conn.execute("NOTIFY pgrst, 'reload schema'")
    return {'applied': done, 'tables_verified': len(CATALOG)}


def record_model(conn, table, model):
    conn.execute('''CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS growpath_meta.embedding_provenance (
        table_name text PRIMARY KEY, model_name text NOT NULL, dimensions int NOT NULL,
        recorded_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now())''')
    old = conn.execute('SELECT model_name FROM growpath_meta.embedding_provenance WHERE table_name=%s',(table,)).fetchone()
    if old and old['model_name'] != model:
        raise ValueError(f'Embedding model mismatch for {table}')
    conn.execute('''INSERT INTO growpath_meta.embedding_provenance(table_name,model_name,dimensions)
        VALUES (%s,%s,1536) ON CONFLICT(table_name) DO NOTHING''',(table,model))


def validate_skill_identity(current, incoming):
    by_id = {r['skill_id']:r for r in current}
    by_name = {r['name']:r['skill_id'] for r in current}
    for row in incoming:
        old = by_id.get(row['skill_id'])
        if (old and any(old[k] != row[k] for k in ('name','category'))) or (row['name'] in by_name and by_name[row['name']] != row['skill_id']):
            raise ValueError('Existing skill IDs/names conflict with the source; migration stopped.')


def upsert_rows(conn, table, rows):
    from psycopg.types.json import Jsonb
    rows = validate_rows(table, rows)
    if not rows:
        return 0
    spec = CATALOG[table]
    fields = [c['column'] for c in spec['columns']]
    if table == 'public.skill_tags':
        validate_skill_identity(conn.execute('SELECT skill_id,name,category FROM public.skill_tags').fetchall(), rows)
    # Different model spaces must never be silently mixed. Provenance is checked by the caller.
    slots = ['%s::'+c['type'] if c['type'].startswith('vector(') else '%s' for c in spec['columns']]
    assignments = ', '.join(f'{ident(c)}=EXCLUDED.{ident(c)}' for c in fields if c not in spec['pk'])
    conflict = 'DO UPDATE SET '+assignments if assignments else 'DO NOTHING'
    statement = f"INSERT INTO {table_sql(table)} ({', '.join(map(ident, fields))}) VALUES ({', '.join(slots)}) ON CONFLICT ({', '.join(map(ident,spec['pk']))}) {conflict}"
    values = []
    for r in rows:
        values.append([Jsonb(r[c['column']]) if c['type']=='jsonb' and r[c['column']] is not None else
                       json.dumps(r[c['column']]) if c['type'].startswith('vector(') and isinstance(r[c['column']], list) else r[c['column']]
                      for c in spec['columns']])
    with conn.cursor() as cur:
        cur.executemany(statement, values)
    return len(rows)


def sync_sequences(conn, tables):
    # Called only for imported tables, after explicit IDs are restored.
    for table in tables:
        for col in CATALOG[table]['columns']:
            seq = conn.execute('SELECT pg_get_serial_sequence(%s,%s) AS seq',(table,col['column'])).fetchone()['seq']
            if seq:
                conn.execute(f'''SELECT setval(%s::regclass,
                    GREATEST(COALESCE((SELECT MAX({ident(col['column'])}) FROM {table_sql(table)}),1),
                    COALESCE(pg_sequence_last_value(%s::regclass),1)), true)''',(seq,seq))


def package_rows(package):
    # The original regulation package remains a nine-table package.
    checksums_path = package / 'report' / 'package_checksums.json'
    if checksums_path.is_file():
        checksums = json.loads(checksums_path.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
        for relative, checksum in checksums.items():
            if digest(inside(package, relative)) != checksum:
                raise ValueError(f'Regulation package checksum mismatch: {relative}')
    else:
        # K-water V11 delivery uses delivery_V11_manifest.json instead of package_checksums.json.
        manifest_path = package / 'delivery_V11_manifest.json'
        if not manifest_path.is_file():
            raise FileNotFoundError(f'Missing regulation package manifest: {manifest_path}')
        manifest = json.loads(manifest_path.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
        checksums = manifest.get('files')
        if not isinstance(checksums, dict):
            raise ValueError('K-water delivery manifest has no files checksum map')
        for relative, checksum in checksums.items():
            relative_path = Path(relative)
            if relative_path.parts and relative_path.parts[0] == package.name:
                target = package.parent / relative_path
            else:
                target = package / relative_path
            if not target.is_file():
                raise FileNotFoundError(f'Manifest file missing: {relative}')
            if digest(target) != str(checksum).lower():
                raise ValueError(f'Regulation package checksum mismatch: {relative}')
    tables = {}
    for table in REGULATION_TABLES:
        path = package / 'data' / f"{table.split('.')[1]}.json"
        if not path.is_file():
            raise FileNotFoundError(f'Missing regulation table file: {path}')
        tables[table] = validate_rows(table, json.loads(path.read_text(encoding='utf-8')))
    return tables

def evaluation_package_rows(package):
    package = Path(package)
    eval_dir = package / '평가기준_추가전처리'
    manifest_path = eval_dir / 'manifest.json'
    if not manifest_path.is_file():
        raise FileNotFoundError(f'Missing evaluation manifest: {manifest_path}')
    manifest = json.loads(manifest_path.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
    if manifest.get('delivery_component') != 'regulation_evaluation_criteria_V01':
        raise ValueError('Unexpected evaluation package component/version')
    files = manifest.get('files')
    if not isinstance(files, dict):
        raise ValueError('Evaluation manifest has no valid files checksum map')
    for relative, expected in files.items():
        path = (eval_dir / relative).resolve()
        if eval_dir.resolve() not in path.parents:
            raise ValueError(f'Invalid path in evaluation manifest: {relative}')
        if not path.is_file():
            raise FileNotFoundError(f'Missing evaluation package file: {relative}')
        if digest(path) != str(expected).lower():
            raise ValueError(f'Evaluation package checksum mismatch: {relative}')
    tables = {}
    for name in ('evaluation_result_schema', 'evaluation_item_definition', 'evaluation_schema_source'):
        path = eval_dir / 'data' / f'{name}.json'
        rows = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
        table = f'hr_regulation_pilot.{name}'
        tables[table] = validate_rows(table, rows)
    return tables


def load_evaluation_criteria(conn, package):
    tables = evaluation_package_rows(package)
    # Parent/reference checks happen before any writes. Missing node IDs are not remapped.
    node_ids = {r['regulation_node_id'] for r in conn.execute(
        'SELECT regulation_node_id FROM hr_regulation_pilot.regulation_node').fetchall()}
    missing_nodes = sorted({r['regulation_node_id'] for r in tables['hr_regulation_pilot.evaluation_schema_source']
                            if r['regulation_node_id'] not in node_ids})
    if missing_nodes:
        raise ValueError(f'Evaluation criteria reference {len(missing_nodes)} regulation_node_id values absent from DB; load matching K-water regulation package first. Examples: {missing_nodes[:10]}')
    check_schema(conn)
    counts = {}
    for table in table_order(tables):
        counts[table] = upsert_rows(conn, table, tables[table])
    return {'loaded': counts, 'review_status_preserved': True, 'skill_id_remapped': False}


def load_regulations(conn, package):
    tables = package_rows(package)
    check_schema(conn)
    counts = {t:upsert_rows(conn,t,tables[t]) for t in table_order(tables)}
    return {'loaded': counts, 'review_states_preserved': True, 'embeddings_generated': False}


def export_model(value):
    if value is None or (isinstance(value,str) and not value.strip()):
        return None
    if not isinstance(value,str) or value.strip().upper() in {'ACTUAL_EMBEDDING_MODEL','UNKNOWN','NONE','NULL'}:
        raise ValueError('Use a verified embedding model name, or omit --embedding-model to back up with unknown provenance.')
    return value.strip()


def require_import_provenance(manifest, tables):
    if export_model(manifest.get('embedding_model')):
        return
    populated = [table for table,rows in tables.items() if any(
        row[col['column']] is not None for row in rows for col in CATALOG[table]['columns']
        if col['type'].startswith('vector('))]
    if populated:
        raise ValueError('Backup is valid, but vector model provenance is unknown for: '+', '.join(populated)+
                         '. Verify the original model or plan re-embedding before importing. No DB changes were made.')


def export_data(conn, output, model=None):
    model = export_model(model)
    if output.exists():
        raise ValueError('Export directory already exists; use a new directory.')
    output.mkdir(parents=True)
    # One repeatable-read snapshot for all tables. Export can run before migrations on the source.
    conn.execute('SET TRANSACTION ISOLATION LEVEL REPEATABLE READ READ ONLY')
    conn.execute('SET LOCAL search_path TO public, extensions')
    conn.execute('SET LOCAL row_security = off')  # Error instead of silently exporting RLS-filtered partial data.
    manifest = {'format_version':2,'kind':'database_export','created_at':datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
                'embedding_model':model,'embedding_model_status':'operator_supplied' if model else 'unknown',
                'tables':[],'missing_tables':[]}
    for table in table_order(TRANSFER_TABLES):
        exists = conn.execute('SELECT to_regclass(%s) AS id',(table,)).fetchone()['id']
        if not exists:
            manifest['missing_tables'].append(table)
            continue
        spec = CATALOG[table]
        filename = table+'.jsonl'
        path, count = output/filename, 0
        existing_cols = {r['column_name'] for r in conn.execute('SELECT column_name FROM information_schema.columns WHERE table_schema=%s AND table_name=%s', tuple(table.split('.'))).fetchall()}
        select_cols = [ident(c['column']) if c['column'] in existing_cols else 'NULL AS '+ident(c['column']) for c in spec['columns']]
        with conn.cursor(name='export_'+table.replace('.','_')) as cur, path.open('w',encoding='utf-8',newline='\n') as stream:
            cur.execute(f"SELECT {', '.join(select_cols)} FROM {table_sql(table)} ORDER BY {', '.join(map(ident,spec['pk']))}")
            for row in cur:
                stream.write(json.dumps(row,ensure_ascii=False,default=str,allow_nan=False)+'\n')
                count += 1
        manifest['tables'].append({'table':table,'file':filename,'rows':count,'sha256':digest(path)})
    # A manifest is written last. An interrupted export is intentionally not importable.
    (output/'manifest.json').write_text(json.dumps(manifest,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2),encoding='utf-8')
    return manifest


def read_bundle(directory):
    manifest = json.loads((directory/'manifest.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
    if manifest.get('format_version') not in (1,2) or manifest.get('kind') != 'database_export':
        raise ValueError('Unsupported export manifest')
    model = export_model(manifest.get('embedding_model'))
    if manifest['format_version'] == 1 and not model:
        raise ValueError('Version 1 bundle is missing embedding model provenance')
    if manifest['format_version'] == 2 and manifest.get('embedding_model_status') != ('operator_supplied' if model else 'unknown'):
        raise ValueError('Embedding model status mismatch')
    manifest['embedding_model'] = model
    tables = {}
    for entry in manifest['tables']:
        table = entry['table']
        if table not in TRANSFER_TABLES or table in tables:
            raise ValueError(f'Unsupported or duplicate transfer table: {table}')
        path = inside(directory,entry['file'])
        if digest(path) != entry['sha256']:
            raise ValueError(f'Checksum mismatch: {table}')
        rows = [json.loads(line) for line in path.read_text(encoding='utf-8').splitlines() if line.strip()]
        if len(rows) != entry['rows']:
            raise ValueError(f'Row count mismatch: {table}')
        tables[table] = validate_rows(table,rows)
    return manifest, tables


def import_data(conn, directory):
    manifest, tables = read_bundle(directory)
    require_import_provenance(manifest,tables)
    check_schema(conn)
    conn.execute('SET LOCAL search_path TO public, extensions')
    counts = {}
    for table in table_order(tables):
        vector_cols = [c['column'] for c in CATALOG[table]['columns'] if c['type'].startswith('vector(')]
        if vector_cols and any(r[c] is not None for r in tables[table] for c in vector_cols):
            old = conn.execute('SELECT to_regclass(%s) AS id',('growpath_meta.embedding_provenance',)).fetchone()['id']
            known = conn.execute('SELECT model_name FROM growpath_meta.embedding_provenance WHERE table_name=%s',(table,)).fetchone() if old else None
            populated = conn.execute(f"SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM {table_sql(table)} WHERE {ident(vector_cols[0])} IS NOT NULL) AS found").fetchone()['found']
            if populated and not known:
                raise ValueError(f'{table} already has vectors with unknown model provenance. Import into an empty target or record verified model provenance first.')
            record_model(conn,table,manifest['embedding_model'])
        counts[table] = upsert_rows(conn,table,tables[table])
    sync_sequences(conn,tables)
    return {'loaded': counts, 'embedding_model':manifest['embedding_model']}


def verify(conn):
    check_schema(conn)
    conn.execute('SET LOCAL search_path TO public, extensions')
    rows = []
    for table in table_order(CATALOG):
        result = conn.execute(f'SELECT COUNT(*) AS count FROM {table_sql(table)}').fetchone()
        result['table'] = table
        for col in CATALOG[table]['columns']:
            if col['type'].startswith('vector('):
                field = ident(col['column'])
                result.update(conn.execute(f'''SELECT COUNT({field}) AS non_null_vectors,
                    COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE vector_norm({field})=0) AS zero_vectors,
                    MIN(vector_dims({field})) AS min_vector_dimensions,
                    MAX(vector_dims({field})) AS max_vector_dimensions FROM {table_sql(table)}''').fetchone())
        rows.append(result)
    return {'tables':rows,'schema_and_foreign_keys':'pass','semantic_search_quality':'not_tested'}


def demo_users(conn):
    check_schema(conn)
    sync_sequences(conn,['public.users'])
    conn.execute((ROOT/'src/db/demo_users.sql').read_text(encoding='utf-8'),prepare=False)
    return {'demo_users':[dict(r) for r in conn.execute("SELECT user_id,name,email FROM public.users WHERE email LIKE '%@growpath.example.invalid'").fetchall()]}


def bootstrap(conn, directory, include_demo=False):
    # Validate the bundle before any schema change. One outer transaction covers the complete bootstrap.
    manifest, tables = read_bundle(directory)
    if not tables.get('public.skill_tags'):
        raise ValueError('Bootstrap requires real skill_tags exported from the source DB.')
    regulatory = set(REGULATION_TABLES)
    supplied = regulatory & tables.keys()
    if supplied and supplied != regulatory:
        raise ValueError('Partial regulation export; include all nine original regulation tables.')
    require_import_provenance(manifest,tables)
    result = {'schema': migrate(conn), 'data': import_data(conn,directory)}
    if not supplied:
        result['regulations'] = load_regulations(conn,PILOT)
    if include_demo:
        result['users'] = demo_users(conn)
    result['verification'] = verify(conn)
    return result


def main(argv=None):
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description='GrowPath DB schema and real-data deployment')
    parser.add_argument('--db-env', default='SUPABASE_DB_URL', help='Environment variable holding PostgreSQL URL')
    commands = parser.add_subparsers(dest='command',required=True)
    commands.add_parser('plan', help='Offline schema/data plan; no DB connection')
    commands.add_parser('migrate', help='Apply missing schema migrations atomically')
    commands.add_parser('verify', help='Read-only schema/FK/count checks')
    reg = commands.add_parser('load-regulations', help='Load verified local regulation JSON')
    reg.add_argument('--package',type=Path,default=PILOT)
    eval_cmd = commands.add_parser('load-evaluation-criteria', help='Load checksum-verified K-water evaluation criteria')
    eval_cmd.add_argument('--package', type=Path, default=PACKAGE)
    exp = commands.add_parser('export-data', help='Read real reference data/vectors from source')
    exp.add_argument('--output',type=Path,required=True)
    exp.add_argument('--embedding-model',nargs='?',default=None,help='Verified source model; omit if unknown (backup allowed, vector import blocked)')
    imp = commands.add_parser('import-data', help='Restore a checked export bundle into destination')
    imp.add_argument('--input',type=Path,required=True)
    commands.add_parser('demo-users', help='Insert only synthetic users/profiles')
    boot = commands.add_parser('bootstrap', help='Schema + real-data bundle + regulation package + verification')
    boot.add_argument('--input',type=Path,required=True)
    boot.add_argument('--demo-users',action='store_true')
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    if args.command == 'export-data':
        args.embedding_model = export_model(args.embedding_model)
    if args.command in ('bootstrap','import-data'):
        manifest, tables = read_bundle(args.input)
        require_import_provenance(manifest,tables)
    if args.command == 'plan':
        local = package_rows(PILOT)
        result = {'database_connection':False,'tables':len(CATALOG),
                  'migrations':[p.name for p in sorted(MIGRATIONS.glob('*.sql'))],
                  'transfer_order':table_order(TRANSFER_TABLES),
                  'local_regulation_rows':{k:len(v) for k,v in local.items()},
                  'skill_and_course_data':'Export from existing DB; no synthetic seeds',
                  'storage_files':'Regulation source files remain in the package; remote Storage upload is a separate deployment task.'}
    else:
        with connect(args.db_env) as conn:
            if args.command == 'migrate':
                result = migrate(conn)
            elif args.command == 'load-regulations':
                result = load_regulations(conn,args.package)
            elif args.command == 'load-evaluation-criteria':
                result = load_evaluation_criteria(conn, args.package)
            elif args.command == 'export-data':
                result = export_data(conn,args.output,args.embedding_model)
            elif args.command == 'import-data':
                result = import_data(conn,args.input)
            elif args.command == 'demo-users':
                result = demo_users(conn)
            elif args.command == 'bootstrap':
                result = bootstrap(conn,args.input,args.demo_users)
            else:
                conn.execute('SET TRANSACTION READ ONLY')
                result = verify(conn)
    print(json.dumps(result,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2,default=str))
    return 0


if __name__ == '__main__':
    try:
        raise SystemExit(main())
    except Exception as exc:
        print(f'{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}', file=sys.stderr)
        raise SystemExit(1)
</file>

<file path="src/db/regulation_vectors.py">
"""Regulation pgvector storage/search. Run: python -m src.db.regulation_vectors --help."""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
from collections import Counter
from datetime import date
import hashlib
import json
import math
import os
from pathlib import Path
import sys

from .deployment import connect

MODEL = 'text-embedding-3-small'
DIMENSIONS = 1536
TABLE = 'hr_regulation_pilot.rag_chunk_embedding'
INPUT_VERSION = 'context_path+blank_line+chunk_text/v1'
DDL = '''
SET LOCAL search_path TO public, extensions;
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS hr_regulation_pilot.rag_chunk_embedding (
    rag_chunk_id VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL REFERENCES hr_regulation_pilot.rag_chunk(rag_chunk_id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    embedding_model TEXT NOT NULL,
    dimensions INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (dimensions = 1536),
    input_version TEXT NOT NULL,
    input_sha256 TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (length(input_sha256) = 64),
    embedding vector(1536) NOT NULL CHECK (vector_norm(embedding) > 0),
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    PRIMARY KEY (rag_chunk_id, embedding_model)
);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS rag_chunk_embedding_model_idx
    ON hr_regulation_pilot.rag_chunk_embedding (embedding_model);
ALTER TABLE hr_regulation_pilot.rag_chunk_embedding ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
REVOKE ALL ON hr_regulation_pilot.rag_chunk_embedding FROM PUBLIC;
DO $$ BEGIN
    IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_roles WHERE rolname='anon') THEN
        REVOKE ALL ON hr_regulation_pilot.rag_chunk_embedding FROM anon;
    END IF;
    IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_roles WHERE rolname='authenticated') THEN
        REVOKE ALL ON hr_regulation_pilot.rag_chunk_embedding FROM authenticated;
    END IF;
    IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_roles WHERE rolname='service_role') THEN
        GRANT USAGE ON SCHEMA hr_regulation_pilot TO service_role;
        GRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE ON hr_regulation_pilot.rag_chunk_embedding TO service_role;
    END IF;
END $$;
'''

# Source data remains authoritative. No review state or original hash is overwritten.
SOURCE_SQL = '''SELECT c.*, v.review_status AS version_review_status,
    v.availability_status, v.effective_from, v.effective_to
    FROM hr_regulation_pilot.rag_chunk c
    JOIN hr_regulation_pilot.regulation_version v USING (regulation_version_id)
    ORDER BY c.rag_chunk_id'''

HASH_SQL = "encode(digest(convert_to(c.context_path || E'\\n\\n' || c.chunk_text,'UTF8'),'sha256'),'hex')"
SEARCH_SQL = '''WITH eligible AS MATERIALIZED (
    SELECT c.rag_chunk_id, c.regulation_version_id, c.chunk_text, c.context_path,
           c.search_status, v.review_status, v.availability_status,
           v.effective_from, v.effective_to, e.embedding
    FROM hr_regulation_pilot.rag_chunk_embedding e
    JOIN hr_regulation_pilot.rag_chunk c USING (rag_chunk_id)
    JOIN hr_regulation_pilot.regulation_version v USING (regulation_version_id)
    WHERE e.embedding_model=%s AND e.dimensions=1536 AND e.input_version=%s
      AND e.input_sha256=''' + HASH_SQL + '''
      AND c.search_status IN ('active','pending_review','needs_review')
      AND (%s OR (c.search_status='active' AND v.review_status='approved'
                  AND v.availability_status='available'))
      AND (%s::text IS NULL OR c.regulation_version_id=%s)
      AND v.effective_from IS NOT NULL AND v.effective_from<=%s
      AND (v.effective_to IS NULL OR v.effective_to>%s)
      AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM hr_regulation_pilot.rag_chunk_source s
                  WHERE s.rag_chunk_id=c.rag_chunk_id)
      AND NOT EXISTS (
          SELECT 1 FROM hr_regulation_pilot.rag_chunk_source s
          JOIN hr_regulation_pilot.regulation_node n USING (regulation_node_id)
          WHERE s.rag_chunk_id=c.rag_chunk_id
            AND ((n.effective_from_override IS NOT NULL AND n.effective_from_override>%s)
              OR (n.effective_to_override IS NOT NULL AND n.effective_to_override<=%s))
      )
), scored AS (
    SELECT *,1-(embedding <=> %s::vector) AS similarity FROM eligible
)
SELECT rag_chunk_id,regulation_version_id,chunk_text,context_path,search_status,
       review_status,availability_status,effective_from,effective_to,similarity
FROM scored WHERE similarity >= %s
ORDER BY similarity DESC,rag_chunk_id LIMIT %s'''


def input_text(row):
    if not isinstance(row['chunk_text'],str) or not row['chunk_text'].strip():
        raise ValueError('Empty regulation text: '+row['rag_chunk_id'])
    return row['context_path']+'\n\n'+row['chunk_text']


def input_hash(row):
    return hashlib.sha256(input_text(row).encode('utf-8')).hexdigest()


def vector_values(value):
    values = json.loads(value) if isinstance(value,str) else value
    if not isinstance(values,list) or len(values)!=DIMENSIONS or any(
        isinstance(x,bool) or not isinstance(x,(int,float)) or not math.isfinite(x) for x in values
    ) or not any(x!=0 for x in values):
        raise ValueError('Invalid regulation vector: expected 1536 finite values and nonzero norm')
    return values


def table_exists(conn):
    return bool(conn.execute('SELECT to_regclass(%s) AS id',(TABLE,)).fetchone()['id'])


def sources(conn):
    if not conn.execute("SELECT to_regclass('hr_regulation_pilot.rag_chunk') AS id").fetchone()['id']:
        raise ValueError('Load the regulation schema and real regulation data first.')
    return conn.execute(SOURCE_SQL).fetchall()


def stored(conn):
    if not table_exists(conn):
        return {}
    return {r['rag_chunk_id']:r for r in conn.execute(
        f'SELECT rag_chunk_id,input_sha256,input_version FROM {TABLE} WHERE embedding_model=%s',
        (MODEL,)).fetchall()}


def needs_embedding(row, previous):
    old = previous.get(row['rag_chunk_id'])
    return not old or old['input_version']!=INPUT_VERSION or old['input_sha256']!=input_hash(row)


def status(conn):
    rows, previous = sources(conn), stored(conn)
    pending = sum(needs_embedding(r,previous) for r in rows)
    return {'source_chunks':len(rows),'vector_table_exists':table_exists(conn),
            'embedding_model':MODEL,'dimensions':DIMENSIONS,'current_vectors':len(rows)-pending,
            'missing_or_stale_vectors':pending,'search_statuses':dict(Counter(r['search_status'] for r in rows)),
            'version_review_statuses':dict(Counter(r['version_review_status'] for r in rows)),
            'database_changes':False,'embedding_api_called':False}


def embed_texts(texts, client=None):
    # UTF-8 byte length is a conservative token upper bound; never silently truncate.
    if not texts or any(not t.strip() or len(t.encode('utf-8'))>8000 for t in texts):
        raise ValueError('Empty or oversized input; re-chunk inputs over 8000 UTF-8 bytes first.')
    if client is None:
        from openai import OpenAI
        key = os.getenv('OPENAI_API_KEY')
        if not key:
            raise ValueError('OPENAI_API_KEY is required for embed/search; status and export do not need it.')
        client = OpenAI(api_key=key,base_url='https://api.openai.com/v1',timeout=30,max_retries=2)
    response = client.embeddings.create(model=MODEL,input=texts,dimensions=DIMENSIONS,encoding_format='float')
    if response.model!=MODEL or len(response.data)!=len(texts):
        raise ValueError('Embedding API model/count mismatch; nothing from this batch was stored.')
    items = sorted(response.data,key=lambda item:item.index)
    if [item.index for item in items]!=list(range(len(texts))):
        raise ValueError('Embedding API response indices do not match the inputs.')
    return [vector_values(item.embedding) for item in items]


def store_rows(conn, entries):
    for entry in entries:
        current = conn.execute('''SELECT rag_chunk_id,chunk_text,context_path
            FROM hr_regulation_pilot.rag_chunk WHERE rag_chunk_id=%s FOR SHARE''',
            (entry['rag_chunk_id'],)).fetchone()
        if current is None or input_hash(current)!=entry['input_sha256']:
            raise ValueError('Regulation source missing/changed; rerun embed: '+entry['rag_chunk_id'])
        vector = json.dumps(vector_values(entry['embedding']),allow_nan=False)
        conn.execute(f'''INSERT INTO {TABLE}
            (rag_chunk_id,embedding_model,dimensions,input_version,input_sha256,embedding)
            VALUES (%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s::vector)
            ON CONFLICT (rag_chunk_id,embedding_model) DO UPDATE SET
              dimensions=EXCLUDED.dimensions,input_version=EXCLUDED.input_version,
              input_sha256=EXCLUDED.input_sha256,embedding=EXCLUDED.embedding,updated_at=now()''',
            (entry['rag_chunk_id'],MODEL,DIMENSIONS,INPUT_VERSION,entry['input_sha256'],vector))


def embed(conn, batch_size=16):
    if not 1<=batch_size<=32:
        raise ValueError('batch-size must be between 1 and 32')
    rows, previous = sources(conn), stored(conn)
    pending = [r for r in rows if needs_embedding(r,previous)]
    if not rows:
        raise ValueError('No real regulation chunks loaded.')
    for row in pending:
        if len(input_text(row).encode('utf-8'))>8000:
            raise ValueError('Re-chunk oversized input before embedding: '+row['rag_chunk_id'])
    if pending and not os.getenv('OPENAI_API_KEY'):
        raise ValueError('OPENAI_API_KEY is required for embedding.')
    conn.execute(DDL,prepare=False)
    conn.commit()
    saved = 0
    for offset in range(0,len(pending),batch_size):
        batch = pending[offset:offset+batch_size]
        vectors = embed_texts([input_text(row) for row in batch])
        with conn.transaction():
            conn.execute('SET LOCAL search_path TO public, extensions')
            store_rows(conn,[{'rag_chunk_id':row['rag_chunk_id'],'input_sha256':input_hash(row),'embedding':vector}
                             for row,vector in zip(batch,vectors)])
        saved += len(batch)
        print(f'Embedded {saved}/{len(pending)} chunks',file=sys.stderr)
    return {'saved':saved,'skipped_unchanged':len(rows)-len(pending),'embedding_model':MODEL,
            'review_statuses_changed':False,'note':'Completed batches are committed; rerun to resume.'}


def search(conn,query,limit=5,threshold=0.25,include_review=False,as_of=None,version_id=None):
    if not 1<=limit<=50 or not math.isfinite(threshold) or not -1<=threshold<=1:
        raise ValueError('limit must be 1..50 and threshold must be finite in -1..1')
    if not table_exists(conn):
        raise ValueError('Run embed to create regulation vectors first.')
    day = as_of or date.today()
    vector = json.dumps(embed_texts([query])[0])
    conn.execute('SET LOCAL search_path TO public, extensions')
    rows = conn.execute(SEARCH_SQL,(MODEL,INPUT_VERSION,include_review,version_id,version_id,
                                   day,day,day,day,vector,threshold,limit)).fetchall()
    for row in rows:
        row['requires_review'] = (row['search_status']!='active' or row['review_status']!='approved'
                                  or row['availability_status']!='available')
        row['sources'] = conn.execute('''SELECT s.regulation_node_id,s.source_role,s.source_span,
            n.label,n.path,n.source_locator,n.extraction_status,n.review_note,
            n.effective_from_override,n.effective_to_override,f.regulation_file_id,f.file_name,f.storage_key
            FROM hr_regulation_pilot.rag_chunk_source s
            JOIN hr_regulation_pilot.regulation_node n USING (regulation_node_id)
            JOIN hr_regulation_pilot.regulation_file f USING (regulation_file_id)
            WHERE s.rag_chunk_id=%s ORDER BY s.source_order''',(row['rag_chunk_id'],)).fetchall()
    return {'matches':rows,'embedding_model':MODEL,'as_of':str(day),
            'review_results_requested':include_review,'no_evidence':not rows}


def export_vectors(conn,output):
    if output.exists():
        raise ValueError('Output file exists; choose a new name.')
    if not table_exists(conn):
        raise ValueError('No regulation vector table; run embed first.')
    conn.execute('SET LOCAL search_path TO public, extensions')
    rows = conn.execute(f'''SELECT e.rag_chunk_id,e.input_sha256,e.embedding::text AS embedding
        FROM {TABLE} e JOIN hr_regulation_pilot.rag_chunk c USING (rag_chunk_id)
        WHERE e.embedding_model=%s AND e.input_version=%s AND e.input_sha256={HASH_SQL}
        ORDER BY e.rag_chunk_id''',(MODEL,INPUT_VERSION)).fetchall()
    if len(rows)!=len(sources(conn)):
        raise ValueError('Some regulation vectors are missing/stale; finish embed before export.')
    for row in rows:
        row['embedding'] = vector_values(row['embedding'])
    payload = {'format_version':1,'kind':'regulation_vectors','model':MODEL,'dimensions':DIMENSIONS,
               'input_version':INPUT_VERSION,'rows':rows}
    data = json.dumps(payload,ensure_ascii=False,sort_keys=True,allow_nan=False)
    wrapper = {'sha256':hashlib.sha256(data.encode('utf-8')).hexdigest(),'payload':data}
    output.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    with output.open('x',encoding='utf-8') as stream:
        json.dump(wrapper,stream,ensure_ascii=False)
    return {'exported_vectors':len(rows),'output':str(output),'model':MODEL}


def import_vectors(conn,path):
    wrapper = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
    data = wrapper['payload']
    if hashlib.sha256(data.encode('utf-8')).hexdigest()!=wrapper['sha256']:
        raise ValueError('Regulation vector export checksum mismatch')
    payload = json.loads(data)
    if (payload['format_version'],payload['kind'],payload['model'],payload['dimensions'],payload['input_version']) != (
            1,'regulation_vectors',MODEL,DIMENSIONS,INPUT_VERSION):
        raise ValueError('Regulation vector export format/model mismatch')
    rows = payload['rows']
    if len({r['rag_chunk_id'] for r in rows})!=len(rows):
        raise ValueError('Duplicate regulation chunk IDs')
    current = {r['rag_chunk_id']:r for r in sources(conn)}
    for row in rows:
        vector_values(row['embedding'])
        if row['rag_chunk_id'] not in current or input_hash(current[row['rag_chunk_id']])!=row['input_sha256']:
            raise ValueError('Restore matching regulation source data before vector import.')
    conn.execute(DDL,prepare=False)
    store_rows(conn,rows)
    return {'imported_vectors':len(rows),'model':MODEL,'embedding_api_called':False}


def main(argv=None):
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument('--db-env',default='SUPABASE_DB_URL')
    commands = parser.add_subparsers(dest='command',required=True)
    commands.add_parser('status',help='Read-only counts and remaining work; no API call')
    emb = commands.add_parser('embed',help='Create vector table and embed real chunks; API charges apply')
    emb.add_argument('--batch-size',type=int,default=16)
    query = commands.add_parser('search',help='Embed query and search the regulation vectors')
    query.add_argument('--query',required=True)
    query.add_argument('--limit',type=int,default=5)
    query.add_argument('--threshold',type=float,default=0.25)
    query.add_argument('--include-review',action='store_true')
    query.add_argument('--as-of',type=date.fromisoformat)
    query.add_argument('--version-id')
    exp = commands.add_parser('export',help='Export regulation vectors separately from db.py source bundle')
    exp.add_argument('--output',type=Path,required=True)
    imp = commands.add_parser('import',help='Restore vectors after restoring matching source regulations')
    imp.add_argument('--input',type=Path,required=True)
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    with connect(args.db_env) as conn:
        if args.command in ('status','search','export'):
            conn.execute('SET TRANSACTION ISOLATION LEVEL REPEATABLE READ READ ONLY')
        if args.command=='status':
            result = status(conn)
        elif args.command=='embed':
            result = embed(conn,args.batch_size)
        elif args.command=='search':
            result = search(conn,args.query,args.limit,args.threshold,args.include_review,args.as_of,args.version_id)
        elif args.command=='export':
            result = export_vectors(conn,args.output)
        else:
            result = import_vectors(conn,args.input)
    print(json.dumps(result,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2,default=str))
    return 0


if __name__=='__main__':
    try:
        raise SystemExit(main())
    except (ValueError,RuntimeError) as exc:
        print(str(exc),file=sys.stderr)
        raise SystemExit(1)
    except Exception as exc:
        # Avoid exposing connection strings, keys, or raw API request data.
        print(f'{type(exc).__name__}: regulation vector operation failed; completed batches remain saved.',file=sys.stderr)
        raise SystemExit(1)

<file path="src/db/resource_vectors.py">
"""Known-model vectors for existing skills and learning resources.

python -m src.db.resource_vectors status
python -m src.db.resource_vectors embed
Original embedding columns and source records are never updated.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import hashlib
import json

from .deployment import connect
# Reuse the already validated OpenAI batch client and vector validator.
from .regulation_vectors import MODEL, DIMENSIONS, embed_texts, vector_values

TABLE = 'public.learning_resource_embedding'
INPUT_VERSION = 'db-resource-fields-and-skill-links/v1'
SPECS = {
    'skills': ('skill_tags', 'skill_id', ('skill_id', 'name', 'category'), None),
    'courses': ('courses', 'course_id', (
        'course_id', 'title', 'category_name', 'org_name', 'url', 'is_matchup',
        'cost', 'duration_weeks', 'recruitment_status', 'syllabus_summary',
        'platform', 'difficulty', 'record_source'), 'course_skill_map'),
    'certifications': ('certifications', 'cert_id', (
        'cert_id', 'title', 'provider', 'test_subjects', 'career_outlook',
        'exam_type', 'pass_score', 'record_source', 'description', 'source_url', 'content_status'), 'cert_skill_map'),
    'books': ('books', 'isbn', (
        'isbn', 'title', 'author', 'publisher', 'kdc_code', 'record_source', 'description', 'source_url', 'content_status'), 'book_skill_map'),
}
DDL = '''
SET LOCAL search_path TO public, extensions;
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.learning_resource_embedding (
    resource_type TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (resource_type IN ('skills','courses','certifications','books')),
    resource_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    embedding_model TEXT NOT NULL,
    dimensions INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (dimensions = 1536),
    input_version TEXT NOT NULL,
    input_sha256 TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (length(input_sha256) = 64),
    embedding vector(1536) NOT NULL CHECK (vector_norm(embedding) > 0),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    PRIMARY KEY (resource_type, resource_id, embedding_model)
);
ALTER TABLE public.learning_resource_embedding ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
REVOKE ALL ON public.learning_resource_embedding FROM PUBLIC;
DO $$ BEGIN
    IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_roles WHERE rolname='anon') THEN
        REVOKE ALL ON public.learning_resource_embedding FROM anon;
    END IF;
    IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_roles WHERE rolname='authenticated') THEN
        REVOKE ALL ON public.learning_resource_embedding FROM authenticated;
    END IF;
    IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_roles WHERE rolname='service_role') THEN
        GRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE ON public.learning_resource_embedding TO service_role;
    END IF;
END $$;
'''


def embedding_text(kind, record):
    """Bound new detail text only; keep the complete source record/hash for grounding."""
    body = dict(record)
    def encode():
        return json.dumps({'resource_type': kind, 'record': body},
                          ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, default=str)
    text = encode()
    if kind in ('books', 'certifications'):
        for field in ('description', 'career_outlook', 'test_subjects'):
            if len(text.encode('utf-8')) <= 8000:
                break
            value = body.get(field)
            if not isinstance(value, str):
                continue
            # Preserve at least 160 characters of each factual field.
            while len(text.encode('utf-8')) > 8000 and len(value) > 160:
                value = value[:max(160, len(value) * 3 // 4)]
                body[field] = value
                text = encode()
    return text


def sources(conn):
    """Only whitelisted real DB fields and real relation rows enter the index."""
    result = {}
    skill_names = {r['skill_id']: r['name'] for r in conn.execute(
        'SELECT skill_id,name FROM public.skill_tags').fetchall()}
    for kind, (table, pk, columns, mapping) in SPECS.items():
        links = {}
        if mapping:
            for row in conn.execute(f'SELECT {pk},skill_id FROM public.{mapping}').fetchall():
                links.setdefault(str(row[pk]), set()).add(row['skill_id'])
        projected = [f"to_jsonb(t)->>'{c}' AS {c}" if c in ('description','source_url','content_status') else f't.{c}' for c in columns]
        for row in conn.execute(f'SELECT {",".join(projected)} FROM public.{table} t ORDER BY {pk}').fetchall():
            key = (kind, str(row[pk]))
            row = dict(row)
            if mapping:
                row['linked_skill_ids'] = sorted(links.get(key[1], set()))
                row['linked_skill_names'] = [skill_names[i] for i in row['linked_skill_ids']]
            text = json.dumps({'resource_type': kind, 'record': row},
                              ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, default=str)
            result[key] = {'record': row, 'text': embedding_text(kind, row),
                           'hash': hashlib.sha256(text.encode('utf-8')).hexdigest()}
    return result


def table_exists(conn):
    return bool(conn.execute('SELECT to_regclass(%s) AS id', (TABLE,)).fetchone()['id'])


def stored(conn):
    if not table_exists(conn):
        return {}
    return {(r['resource_type'], r['resource_id']): r for r in conn.execute(
        f'''SELECT resource_type,resource_id,input_sha256,input_version,dimensions
            FROM {TABLE} WHERE embedding_model=%s''', (MODEL,)).fetchall()}


def is_current(source, previous):
    return bool(previous and previous['input_sha256'] == source['hash']
                and previous['input_version'] == INPUT_VERSION
                and previous['dimensions'] == DIMENSIONS)


def status(conn):
    rows, previous = sources(conn), stored(conn)
    counts = {}
    for kind in SPECS:
        subset = {k: v for k, v in rows.items() if k[0] == kind}
        current = sum(is_current(v, previous.get(k)) for k, v in subset.items())
        counts[kind] = {'source_count': len(subset), 'current_vectors': current,
                        'missing_or_stale_vectors': len(subset) - current}
    return {'embedding_model': MODEL, 'dimensions': DIMENSIONS,
            'vector_table_exists': table_exists(conn), 'resources': counts,
            'orphan_vectors_ignored': len(set(previous) - set(rows)),
            'database_changes': False, 'embedding_api_called': False}


def embed(conn, client=None):
    """Atomic, repeatable ingestion; missing/stale records only, batches of 32."""
    with conn.transaction():
        conn.execute(DDL)
        rows, previous = sources(conn), stored(conn)
        pending = [(k, v) for k, v in rows.items() if not is_current(v, previous.get(k))]
        # Fail before any paid call when the source needs explicit re-chunking.
        if any(len(v['text'].encode('utf-8')) > 8000 for _, v in pending):
            raise ValueError('Resource text exceeds 8000 UTF-8 bytes; review the source before indexing.')
        for start in range(0, len(pending), 32):
            batch = pending[start:start + 32]
            vectors = embed_texts([v['text'] for _, v in batch], client=client)
            for ((kind, rid), row), vector in zip(batch, vectors):
                conn.execute(f'''INSERT INTO {TABLE}
                    (resource_type,resource_id,embedding_model,dimensions,input_version,input_sha256,embedding)
                    VALUES (%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s::vector)
                    ON CONFLICT (resource_type,resource_id,embedding_model) DO UPDATE SET
                    dimensions=EXCLUDED.dimensions,input_version=EXCLUDED.input_version,
                    input_sha256=EXCLUDED.input_sha256,embedding=EXCLUDED.embedding,updated_at=now()''',
                    (kind, rid, MODEL, DIMENSIONS, INPUT_VERSION, row['hash'], json.dumps(vector)))
        if {k: v['hash'] for k, v in sources(conn).items()} != {k: v['hash'] for k, v in rows.items()}:
            raise ValueError('DB sources changed during embedding. Rolled back; retry with stable sources.')
        result = status(conn)
        result.update(embedded_count=len(pending), database_changes=True,
                      embedding_api_called=bool(pending), original_embeddings_changed=False)
        return result


def similarities(conn, rows, keys, query_vector):
    """Exact cosine search over current, real source rows; no unknown legacy vectors."""
    previous = stored(conn)
    keys = set(keys)
    if any(k not in rows or not is_current(rows[k], previous.get(k)) for k in keys):
        raise ValueError('학습 자원 벡터가 없거나 오래되었습니다. python -m src.db.resource_vectors embed 를 실행하세요.')
    if not keys:
        return {}
    vector = json.dumps(vector_values(query_vector))
    # 138 rows currently: exact pgvector search avoids ANN recall loss.
    conn.execute('SET LOCAL search_path TO public, extensions')
    scored = conn.execute(f'''SELECT resource_type,resource_id,
        1-(embedding <=> %s::vector) AS similarity FROM {TABLE}
        WHERE embedding_model=%s AND dimensions=%s AND input_version=%s''',
        (vector, MODEL, DIMENSIONS, INPUT_VERSION)).fetchall()
    return {(r['resource_type'], r['resource_id']): float(r['similarity'])
            for r in scored if (r['resource_type'], r['resource_id']) in keys}


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument('--db-env', default='SUPABASE_DB_URL')
    parser.add_argument('command', choices=('status', 'embed'))
    args = parser.parse_args()
    try:
        with connect(args.db_env) as conn:
            if args.command == 'status':
                conn.execute('SET TRANSACTION READ ONLY')
            result = status(conn) if args.command == 'status' else embed(conn)
        print(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))
    except Exception as exc:
        # Connection errors can include host/credentials; do not dump raw exceptions.
        print(json.dumps({'status': 'FAILED', 'error_type': type(exc).__name__,
                          'message': 'DB 연결·원본 테이블·OPENAI_API_KEY·임베딩 한도를 확인하세요. 기존 데이터는 유지됩니다.'},
                         ensure_ascii=False))
        raise SystemExit(1)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()

<file path="src/db/schema.sql">
-- =====================================================================
-- GrowPath AI 통합 데이터베이스 스키마 (schema.sql)
-- =====================================================================

CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS "uuid-ossp";
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS vector;

-- 1. [기존 검증 통과용] 스킬 태그 마스터 테이블
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS skill_tags (
    skill_id BIGINT PRIMARY KEY,
    name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    category VARCHAR(50) DEFAULT 'IT/SW',
    embedding VECTOR(1536),
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- 2. [기존 검증 통과용] 강좌 마스터 테이블
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS courses (
    course_id VARCHAR(50) PRIMARY KEY,
    title VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    category_name VARCHAR(100),
    org_name VARCHAR(100),
    url TEXT NOT NULL,
    is_matchup BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    cost NUMERIC(10, 2) DEFAULT 0.00,
    duration_weeks INT DEFAULT 4,
    recruitment_status VARCHAR(50) DEFAULT '상시학습',
    syllabus_summary TEXT,
    platform VARCHAR(50) DEFAULT 'K-MOOC',
    difficulty VARCHAR(30) DEFAULT 'INTERMEDIATE',
    record_source VARCHAR(50) DEFAULT 'REAL_PUBLIC_DATA',
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- 3. [기존 검증 통과용] 프롬프트 버전 관리 테이블
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS prompt_versions (
    prompt_version_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    agent_id VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    version_num INT NOT NULL,
    system_prompt TEXT NOT NULL,
    change_reason TEXT,
    parent_version_id UUID REFERENCES prompt_versions(prompt_version_id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    deactivated_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    CONSTRAINT uq_agent_version UNIQUE (agent_id, version_num)
);

-- 4. [기존 검증 통과 및 사용자 마스터] users 테이블
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users (
    user_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    username VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    email VARCHAR(255) UNIQUE,
    department VARCHAR(100),
    job_title VARCHAR(100),
    grade VARCHAR(50),
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- 5. [신규 7단계 1] 최상위 프로젝트 로드맵 세션 테이블
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS roadmap_sessions (
    session_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    user_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(user_id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    project_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    status VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'DRAFT',
    current_stage INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    created_at TIMESTAMP NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMP NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- 6. [신규 7단계 2] 1~2단계 프로젝트 계획 산출물
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS project_plans (
    plan_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    session_id UUID NOT NULL UNIQUE REFERENCES roadmap_sessions(session_id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    draft_plan TEXT,
    refined_plan TEXT,
    project_goal TEXT,
    project_scope TEXT,
    tech_stack JSONB DEFAULT '[]'::jsonb,
    milestones JSONB DEFAULT '[]'::jsonb,
    key_issues JSONB DEFAULT '[]'::jsonb,
    deliverables JSONB DEFAULT '[]'::jsonb,
    validation_criteria JSONB DEFAULT '[]'::jsonb,
    created_at TIMESTAMP NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMP NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    CONSTRAINT uq_session_project_plan UNIQUE (session_id)
);

-- 7. [신규 7단계 3] 2단계 Socratic 대화 감사 로그
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS plan_conversations (
    conversation_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    session_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES roadmap_sessions(session_id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    role VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL CHECK (role IN ('user', 'assistant', 'system')),
    content TEXT NOT NULL,
    sequence_no INT NOT NULL,
    created_at TIMESTAMP NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- 8. [신규 7단계 4] 3단계/6단계 동적 진단 문항
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS assessment_questions (
    question_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    session_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES roadmap_sessions(session_id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    assessment_type VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL CHECK (assessment_type IN ('PRE', 'POST')),
    skill VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    question TEXT NOT NULL,
    options JSONB DEFAULT '[]'::jsonb,
    sequence_no INT NOT NULL,
    created_at TIMESTAMP NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- 9. [신규 7단계 5] 3단계/6단계 진단 응답 및 숙련도 등급
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS assessment_responses (
    response_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    session_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES roadmap_sessions(session_id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    question_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES assessment_questions(question_id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    assessment_type VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL CHECK (assessment_type IN ('PRE', 'POST')),
    selected_option TEXT NOT NULL,
    score INT NOT NULL,
    answered_at TIMESTAMP NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    CONSTRAINT uq_session_question_resp UNIQUE (session_id, question_id)
);

-- 10. [신규 7단계 6] 4단계 3대 대안 로드맵
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS roadmap_generations (
    generation_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    session_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES roadmap_sessions(session_id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    strategy_type VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    strategy_title VARCHAR(255),
    summary TEXT,
    roadmap_content JSONB NOT NULL,
    generation_order INT,
    created_at TIMESTAMP NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- 11. [신규 7단계 7] 5단계 로드맵 채택 결과
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS roadmap_selections (
    selection_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    session_id UUID NOT NULL UNIQUE REFERENCES roadmap_sessions(session_id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    generation_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES roadmap_generations(generation_id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    selected_at TIMESTAMP NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    CONSTRAINT uq_session_roadmap_selection UNIQUE (session_id)
);

-- 12. [신규 7단계 8] 5단계 채택 로드맵 상세 설계 보고서
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS roadmap_reports (
    report_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    session_id UUID NOT NULL UNIQUE REFERENCES roadmap_sessions(session_id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    generation_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES roadmap_generations(generation_id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    report_content JSONB NOT NULL,
    created_at TIMESTAMP NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMP NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    CONSTRAINT uq_session_roadmap_report UNIQUE (session_id)
);

-- 13. [신규 7단계 9] 7단계 종합 성장 평가 보고서
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS assessment_reports (
    report_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    session_id UUID NOT NULL UNIQUE REFERENCES roadmap_sessions(session_id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    report_type VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PRE_POST',
    report_content JSONB NOT NULL,
    created_at TIMESTAMP NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    CONSTRAINT uq_session_assessment_report UNIQUE (session_id)
);

-- 인덱스
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_sessions_user ON roadmap_sessions(user_id, status);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_conv_session_seq ON plan_conversations(session_id, sequence_no ASC);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_questions_session ON assessment_questions(session_id, assessment_type, sequence_no ASC);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_responses_session ON assessment_responses(session_id, assessment_type);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_generations_session ON roadmap_generations(session_id, generation_order ASC);

-- [추후 정식 도입 시 추가] 14. 사내 규정집 및 15. AI 구축 사례 RAG 테이블

-- 14. 사내 교육/기술 규정집 테이블
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS regulations (
    id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    doc_title VARCHAR(150) NOT NULL,
    article_no VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    category VARCHAR(50) DEFAULT '교육훈련',
    content TEXT NOT NULL,
    embedding vector(1536),
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- 15. 사내 AI/데이터 구축 사례 테이블
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS ai_cases (
    id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    title VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
    department VARCHAR(100),
    solution_summary TEXT NOT NULL,
    embedding vector(1536),
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- RAG 검색 가속을 위한 HNSW 벡터 인덱스
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_regulations_embedding_hnsw
ON regulations USING hnsw (embedding vector_cosine_ops)
WITH (m = 16, ef_construction = 64);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_ai_cases_embedding_hnsw
ON ai_cases USING hnsw (embedding vector_cosine_ops)
WITH (m = 16, ef_construction = 64);

<file path="src/pipelines/__init__.py">
